48	- L2049 算出 `tx,ty` 后：`smartWanted && smart.showing → {tx,ty} = smart target`（挖掘 :2170 tryMine、放置 tryPlace/tryPlaceWall、右键交互共用同一对 tx,ty——注意**右键交互门**：让位集内格右键仍走原交互，智能光标只影响左键使用）。
49	- 每帧（updateUse 头部）跑一次 `smartLookup`；`hover` 传渲染时附 `{x,y,smart:true}`。
50	- Ctrl 输入：`inp.down('ControlLeft')` + 帧间 prev 状态做按下沿；切换播音效 `menuTick`。
51	
52	### 4. 渲染（Renderer.ts）
53	
54	- hover 框 smart 态：原版 `DrawSmartCursor`（Main.cs:46016-46066）——目标格四象限 8×8 块 `(1,0.9,0.1)×0.6` 黄 + 2px 外框十字 `(1,0.95,0.3)`，颜色乘该格光照；有方向锁时画方向箭头（8 方向，沿方向偏移 16/20px + 0.9s 正弦浮动——先用 canvas 三角形绘制，SmartCursorArrow 贴图后补）。
55	- 鼠标指针：查现有光标图集（vanilla 资源条）是否有 Cursors[12] 帧，有则切换、无则保持现指针+目标格高亮（后补贴图白名单）。
56	
57	### 5. 设置项（对齐原版设置结构 Main.cs:49705）
58	
59	- 设置 UI（语言设置同屏或新页）加：智能光标模式（Toggle/Hold，默认 Toggle）+ `SmartAxeAfterPickaxe` / `SmartBlocksEnabled` / `HoldCanReleaseMidUse` 三开关（默认 false/true/true）。
60	- 持久化走现有设置存储（localStorage，同语言设置模式）。
61	
62	## 修改文件
63	
64	- 新建 `src/player/SmartCursor.ts`（状态机+策略链 39 个+射线/扫描骨架）
65	- `src/core/Game.ts`（L2049 覆盖点、每帧 lookup、Ctrl 输入沿、hover 附 smart）
66	- `src/render/Renderer.ts`（smart 高亮画法 + 可选指针帧）
67	- 设置 UI 文件（模式+三开关）
68	- 测试 `tests/smart-cursor.test.ts`：状态机两模式、镐射线（洞穴穿墙选下一块）、斧定位树根、火把最近合法位+去重、BlocksFilling ≥2 排序、平台方向锁续铺、让位集（宝箱格不劫持）、镐不选平台
69	
70	## 验证
71	
72	1. `npx tsc --noEmit` 零错误 + `npx vitest run` 全绿（新增测试 ~10 例）。
73	2. E2E 探针（scripts/_smart-cursor-probe.mjs）：进游戏持镐站洞穴边按 Ctrl → 断言 `game.smartCursor.showing` 且目标格为射线首个实心非平台块；持斧指向树冠 → 目标=树根格；持火把空房 → 目标=最近合法锚位；Toggle 按一次开/再按关。
74	3. 目检：黄框高亮出现在目标格（而非鼠标格）、方向箭头随平台续铺浮动。
75	
76	## 备注
77	
78	- 反编译两处存疑（MineShinies :2315 循环、StaffOfRegrowth :228 条件）按调研标注以运行行为校准。
79	- Smart Select（Shift 自动换工具，Player.cs:17283）是独立系统，本批不做（不在智能光标范围）。

167		private static void TryFindingPaintInplayerInventory(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, out int paintLookup, out int coatingLookup)
168		{
169			_ = providedInfo.player.inventory;
170			paintLookup = 0;
171			coatingLookup = 0;
172			if (providedInfo.item.type == 1071 || providedInfo.item.type == 1543 || providedInfo.item.type == 1072 || providedInfo.item.type == 1544)
173			{
174				Item item = providedInfo.player.FindPaintOrCoating();
175				if (item != null)
176				{
177					coatingLookup = item.paintCoating;
178					paintLookup = item.paint;
179				}
180			}
181		}
182	
183		private static bool IsHoveringOverAnInteractableTileThatBlocksSmartCursor(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo)
184		{
185			bool result = false;
186			Tile tile = Main.tile[providedInfo.screenTargetX, providedInfo.screenTargetY];
187			if (tile.active())
188			{
189				if (TileID.Sets.DisableSmartCursor[tile.type])
190				{
191					result = true;
192				}
193				if (tile.type == 314 && providedInfo.player.gravDir == 1f)
194				{
195					result = true;
196				}
197			}
198			return result;
199		}
200	
201		private static bool AllowNormalBlockPlacementBehaviourForItemType(int itemType)
202		{
203			if (itemType < 0 || itemType >= ItemID.Count)
204			{
205				return false;
206			}
207			if (itemType == 213 || itemType == 5295 || ItemID.Sets.GrassSeeds[itemType] || ItemID.Sets.Moss[itemType])
208			{
209				return false;
210			}
211			return true;
212		}
213	
214		private static void Step_StaffOfRegrowth(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, ref int focusedX, ref int focusedY)
215		{
216			if ((providedInfo.item.type != 213 && providedInfo.item.type != 5295) || focusedX != -1 || focusedY != -1)
217			{
218				return;
219			}
220			_targets.Clear();
221			for (int i = providedInfo.reachableStartX; i <= providedInfo.reachableEndX; i++)
222			{
223				for (int j = providedInfo.reachableStartY; j <= providedInfo.reachableEndY; j++)
224				{
225					Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
226					bool flag = !Main.tile[i - 1, j].active() || !Main.tile[i, j + 1].active() || !Main.tile[i + 1, j].active() || !Main.tile[i, j - 1].active();
227					bool flag2 = !Main.tile[i - 1, j - 1].active() || !Main.tile[i - 1, j + 1].active() || !Main.tile[i + 1, j + 1].active() || !Main.tile[i + 1, j - 1].active();
228					if (tile.active() && !tile.inActive() && tile.type == 0 && (flag || (tile.type == 0 && flag2)))
229					{
230						_targets.Add(new Point(i, j));
231					}
232				}
233			}
234			if (_targets.Count > 0)
235			{
236				float num = -1f;
237				Point point = _targets[0];
238				for (int k = 0; k < _targets.Count; k++)
239				{
240					float num2 = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(_targets[k].X, _targets[k].Y) * 16f + Vector2.One * 8f, providedInfo.mouse);
241					if (num == -1f || num2 < num)
242					{
243						num = num2;
244						point = _targets[k];
245					}
246				}
247				if (Collision.InTileBounds(point.X, point.Y, providedInfo.reachableStartX, providedInfo.reachableStartY, providedInfo.reachableEndX, providedInfo.reachableEndY))
248				{
249					focusedX = point.X;
250					focusedY = point.Y;
251				}
252			}
253			_targets.Clear();
254		}
255	
256		private static void Step_GrassSeeds(SmartCursorUsageInfo providedInfo, ref int focusedX, ref int focusedY)
257		{
258			if (focusedX > -1 || focusedY > -1)
259			{
260				return;
261			}
262			int type = providedInfo.item.type;
263			if (type < 0 || type >= ItemID.Count || !ItemID.Sets.GrassSeeds[type])
264			{
265				return;
266			}
267			_targets.Clear();
268			for (int i = providedInfo.reachableStartX; i <= providedInfo.reachableEndX; i++)
269			{
270				for (int j = providedInfo.reachableStartY; j <= providedInfo.reachableEndY; j++)
271				{

1403		private static bool AllowedForContinuity(int x, int y, int skipsAllowed)
1404		{
1405			if (!_lockedContinuityCoords.HasValue)
1406			{
1407				return true;
1408			}
1409			Point value = _lockedContinuityCoords.Value;
1410			if (x == value.X && y == value.Y)
1411			{
1412				return true;
1413			}
1414			if (!_lockedDesiredDirection.HasValue)
1415			{
1416				return false;
1417			}
1418			for (int i = 0; i < skipsAllowed; i++)
1419			{
1420				value.X += _lockedDesiredDirection.Value.X;
1421				value.Y += _lockedDesiredDirection.Value.Y;
1422				if (x == value.X && y == value.Y)
1423				{
1424					return true;
1425				}
1426			}
1427			return false;
1428		}

1430		private static Point GetDesiredDirectionFrom(Vector2 offset)
1431		{
1432			if (_lockedDesiredDirection.HasValue)
1433			{
1434				return _lockedDesiredDirection.Value;
1435			}
1436			float num = offset.ToRotation();
1437			if (num < 0f)
1438			{
1439				num += MathF.PI * 2f;
1440			}
1441			float num2 = MathF.PI / 4f;
1442			return (((float)(int)((num + num2 / 2f) % (MathF.PI * 2f) / num2) * num2).ToRotationVector2() * 1.5f).ToPoint();
1443		}

2867		private static bool IsValidSpotForTorch(int x, int y, Tile tileCache)

2878			if (tile.active() && (tile.slope() == 0 || tile.slope() % 2 != 1) && ((Main.tileSolid[tile.type] && !Main.tileNoAttach[tile.type] && !Main.tileSolidTop[tile.type] && !TileID.Sets.NotReallySolid[tile.type]) || TileID.Sets.IsBeam[tile.type] || (WorldGen.IsTreeType(tile.type) && WorldGen.IsTreeType(Main.tile[x - 1, y - 1].type) && WorldGen.IsTreeType(Main.tile[x - 1, y + 1].type))))

2889			{

2891			}

public struct TileReachCheckSettings
{
	public int TileRangeMultiplier;

	public int? OverrideYReach;

	public static readonly TileReachCheckSettings Simple = new TileReachCheckSettings
	{
		TileRangeMultiplier = 1,
		TileReachLimit = 20
	};

	public static readonly TileReachCheckSettings Pylons = new TileReachCheckSettings
	{
		OverrideXReach = 60,
		OverrideYReach = 60
	};

	public void GetRanges(out int x, out int y)
	{
		x = Player.tileRangeX * TileRangeMultiplier;
		y = Player.tileRangeY * TileRangeMultiplier;
		if (TileReachLimit.HasValue)
		{
			if (x > TileReachLimit.Value)
			{
				x = TileReachLimit.Value;
			}
			if (y > TileReachLimit.Value)
			{
				y = TileReachLimit.Value;
			}
		}
		if (OverrideXReach.HasValue)
		{
			x = OverrideXReach.Value;
		}
		if (OverrideYReach.HasValue)
		{
			y = OverrideYReach.Value;
		}
	}

	public void GetTileRegion(Player player, out int LX, out int LY, out int HX, out int HY, int TB = 0)
	{
		GetRanges(out var x, out var y);
		x += TB;
		y += TB;
		LX = (int)(player.position.X / 16f) - x;
		HX = (int)Math.Ceiling((player.position.X + (float)player.width) / 16f) - 1 + x;
		LY = (int)(player.position.Y / 16f) - y;
		HY = (int)Math.Ceiling((player.position.Y + (float)player.height) / 16f) - 1 + y;
	}

	public Rectangle GetTileRegion(Player player, int TB = 0)
	{
		GetTileRegion(player, out var LX, out var LY, out var HX, out var HY, TB);
		return new Rectangle(LX, LY, HX - LX, HY - LY);
	}

	public void GetWorldRegion(Player player, out int LX, out int LY, out int HX, out int HY, int TB = 0)
	{
		GetTileRegion(player, out LX, out LY, out HX, out HY, TB);
		LX *= 16;
		LY *= 16;
		HX *= 16;
		HY *= 16;
		HX += 15;
		HY += 15;
	}

	public Rectangle GetWorldRegion(Player player, int TB = 0)
	{
		GetWorldRegion(player, out var LX, out var LY, out var HX, out var HY, TB);
		return new Rectangle(LX, LY, HX - LX, HY - LY);
	}
}

```

1	// 键盘/鼠标输入。UI 打开时屏蔽游戏输入。
2	export class Input {
3	  keys = new Set<string>();
4	  mouseX = 0; mouseY = 0;
5	  mouseDown = false;
6	  rightDown = false;
7	  uiBlocking = false;     // 背包/合成等打开时为 true
8	  wheelDelta = 0;
9	  keydownHandlers: Array<(code: string) => void> = [];
10	
11	  private el: HTMLElement;
12	  /** 已注册监听(destroy 时全部移除——window 强引用曾把整个旧 Game 钉死,
13	   *  连续多次读档 GPU 内存累积 → contextlost 风暴卡死,2026-08-10 trace 实证) */
14	  private winListeners: Array<[string, EventListener, AddEventListenerOptions?]> = [];
15	  private elListeners: Array<[string, EventListener, AddEventListenerOptions?]> = [];
16	
17	  constructor(el: HTMLElement) {
18	    this.el = el;
19	    const onWin = (type: string, fn: EventListener, opts?: AddEventListenerOptions) => {
20	      window.addEventListener(type, fn, opts);
21	      this.winListeners.push([type, fn, opts]);
22	    };
23	    const onEl = (type: string, fn: EventListener, opts?: AddEventListenerOptions) => {
24	      el.addEventListener(type, fn, opts);
25	      this.elListeners.push([type, fn, opts]);
26	    };
27	
28	    onWin('keydown', ((e: KeyboardEvent) => {
29	      if (e.repeat) return;
30	      this.keys.add(e.code);
31	      this.keydownHandlers.forEach((fn) => fn(e.code));
32	      if (['Space', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Tab'].includes(e.code)) e.preventDefault();
33	    }) as EventListener);
34	    onWin('keyup', ((e: KeyboardEvent) => { this.keys.delete(e.code); }) as EventListener);
35	    onWin('blur', (() => this.keys.clear()) as EventListener);
36	    onEl('mousemove', ((e: MouseEvent) => {
37	      const r = this.el.getBoundingClientRect();
38	      this.mouseX = e.clientX - r.left;
39	      this.mouseY = e.clientY - r.top;
40	    }) as EventListener);
41	    // mousedown 挂 window：不依赖事件目标是否恰好是 canvas
42	    onWin('mousedown', ((e: MouseEvent) => {
43	      // UI 面板上的点击不穿透为游戏输入
44	      if ((e.target as HTMLElement)?.closest?.('.sw-panel, .sw-slot, .sw-recipe, .sw-btn')) return;
45	      if (e.button === 0) this.mouseDown = true;
46	      if (e.button === 2) this.rightDown = true;
47	    }) as EventListener);
48	    onWin('mouseup', ((e: MouseEvent) => {
49	      if (e.button === 0) this.mouseDown = false;
50	      if (e.button === 2) this.rightDown = false;
51	    }) as EventListener);
52	    onEl('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());
53	    onEl('wheel', ((e: WheelEvent) => {
54	      this.wheelDelta += Math.sign(e.deltaY);
55	      e.preventDefault();
56	    }) as EventListener, { passive: false });
57	  }
58	
59	  /** 移除全部监听(退出世界时 Game.destroy 调用) */
60	  destroy(): void {
61	    for (const [t, fn, opts] of this.winListeners) window.removeEventListener(t, fn, opts);
62	    for (const [t, fn, opts] of this.elListeners) this.el.removeEventListener(t, fn, opts);
63	    this.winListeners = [];
64	    this.elListeners = [];
65	  }
66	
67	  down(code: string): boolean {
68	    return !this.uiBlocking && this.keys.has(code);
69	  }
70	
71	  /** 每帧末调用，处理一次性输入（滚轮切换快捷栏） */
72	  endFrame() {
73	    this.wheelDelta = 0;
74	  }
75	}
76

158	  }
159	
160	  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
161	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
162	    this.wall[this.idx(x, y)] = wall;
163	  }
164	
165	  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
166	   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
167	  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
168	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
169	    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
170	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
171	  }
172	
173	  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
174	    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
175	  }
176	
177	  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
178	  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
179	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
180	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
181	    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
182	    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
183	    this.wire[i] = b;
184	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
185	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
186	  }
187	
188	  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
189	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
190	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
191	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
192	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
193	    this.liquid[i] = a;
194	    this.liquidType[i] = type;
195	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
196	  }
197	}

1	// 相机：跟随玩家 + 缓动 + 世界边界钳制
2	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	
4	export class Camera {
5	  x = 0; y = 0;         // 视野中心（世界 px）
6	  zoom = 1.25;          // 当前缩放（向 targetZoom 缓动）
7	  zoomTarget = 1.25;    // 目标缩放
8	  static ZOOM_MIN = 0.75;
9	  static ZOOM_MAX = 2.5;
10	  /** 每帧把 zoom 向 target 缓动（指数趋近，永不到达导致卡死） */
11	  easeZoom() {
12	    const diff = this.zoomTarget - this.zoom;
13	    if (Math.abs(diff) < 0.0005) { this.zoom = this.zoomTarget; return; }
14	    this.zoom += diff * 0.18;
15	  }
16	  setZoomTarget(z: number) {
17	    this.zoomTarget = Math.max(Camera.ZOOM_MIN, Math.min(Camera.ZOOM_MAX, z));
18	  }
19	  viewW = 800; viewH = 600; // 屏幕尺寸
20	
21	  constructor(public worldW: number, public worldH: number) {}
22	
23	  follow(targetX: number, targetY: number) {
24	    this.x += (targetX - this.x) * 0.15;
25	    this.y += (targetY - this.y) * 0.15;
26	  }
27	
28	  clamp() {
29	    const halfW = this.viewW / 2 / this.zoom;
30	    const halfH = this.viewH / 2 / this.zoom;
31	    this.x = Math.max(halfW, Math.min(this.worldW * TILE - halfW, this.x));
32	    this.y = Math.max(halfH, Math.min(this.worldH * TILE - halfH, this.y));
33	    // 世界比视野小的情况
34	    if (this.worldW * TILE < halfW * 2) this.x = this.worldW * TILE / 2;
35	    if (this.worldH * TILE < halfH * 2) this.y = this.worldH * TILE / 2;
36	  }
37	
38	  worldToScreen(wx: number, wy: number): [number, number] {
39	    return [(wx - this.x) * this.zoom + this.viewW / 2, (wy - this.y) * this.zoom + this.viewH / 2];
40	  }
41	  screenToWorld(sx: number, sy: number): [number, number] {
42	    return [(sx - this.viewW / 2) / this.zoom + this.x, (sy - this.viewH / 2) / this.zoom + this.y];
43	  }
44	  tileUnder(sx: number, sy: number): [number, number] {
45	    const [wx, wy] = this.screenToWorld(sx, sy);
46	    return [Math.floor(wx / TILE), Math.floor(wy / TILE)];
47	  }
48	}
49

3	//   frameX 0 = 底锚直立（地面/背景墙共用直立贴图）、22 = 左锚（贴左块倾斜）、44 = 右锚。
4	//   +66 起为未点燃变体（Torch God 熄灭，未移植）。
5	// TileObjectData.cs:4584-4602 锚定义：
6	//   base      AnchorBottom = SolidTile | SolidSide
7	//   alt[1]    AnchorLeft   = SolidTile | SolidSide | Tree | AlternateTile(124,561,574-578)
8	//   alt[2]    AnchorRight  = 同上
9	//   alt[0]    AnchorWall   = 任意背景墙
10	// CanPlace（TileObject.cs:203 while 循环）按 base → alt[1] → alt[2] → alt[0] 顺序尝试，全过即取。

14	/** 树干/棕榈干 sheet（AnchorLeft/Right 的 Tree|AlternateTile 项） */

16	
17	const isTrunk = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean => {
18	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
19	  if (!st.flags[i] || st.type[i] === 0) return false;
20	  const sheet = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]]?.vanilla?.sheet;
21	  return sheet !== undefined && TRUNK_SHEETS.has(sheet);
22	};
23	
24	/** 可锚定的支撑（AnchorLeft/Right 用：实心或树干） */
25	const anchorable = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean =>
26	  st.isSolid(x, y) || isTrunk(st, x, y);
27	
28	/**
29	 * 计算放置锚点帧。优先级 1:1 TileObject.CanPlace：底锚(0) → 左锚(22) → 右锚(44) → 墙锚(0)。
30	 * 无任何锚点返回 -1（放置拒绝）。
31	 */
32	export function torchAnchorFrame(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number): number {
33	  if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return -1;
34	  if (st.isSolid(tx, ty + 1)) return 0;                       // base: AnchorBottom
35	  if (anchorable(st, tx - 1, ty)) return 22;                  // alt[1]: AnchorLeft
36	  if (anchorable(st, tx + 1, ty)) return 44;                  // alt[2]: AnchorRight
37	  if (st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)] !== 0) return 0;                // alt[0]: AnchorWall
38	  return -1;
39	}
40	
41	/**
42	 * 已放置火把的锚是否仍然成立（支撑被挖掉时判定掉落）。
43	 * frameX 0 的直立火把：底锚或墙锚任一在即成立（vanilla 行为：拆地板留墙不掉）。
44	 */
45	export function torchStillAnchored(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number, frameX: number): boolean {
46	  if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return false;
47	  switch (frameX) {
48	    case 22: return anchorable(st, tx - 1, ty);
49	    case 44: return anchorable(st, tx + 1, ty);
50	    default: return st.isSolid(tx, ty + 1) || st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)] !== 0;
51	  }
52	}

// 内部 tile / wall 注册表。id 即数组下标。
// 数值（硬度/光照）为原创平衡设计；mapColor 参考 TEdit tiles.json 的地图配色后重新取整。

export interface TileDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  solid: boolean;        // 参与碰撞
  platform: boolean;     // 单向平台（下跳可穿）
  decor: boolean;        // 装饰物（不碰撞、可被任何工具秒清）
  framed: boolean;       // 多格框架物体（使用 frameX/frameY 定位贴图）
  opacity: number;       // 光照阻挡 0-1
  light?: [number, number, number]; // 自发光 RGB
  pick: number;          // 所需镐力（-1 不可挖）
  axe: number;           // 所需斧力
  mapColor: string;      // 小地图颜色
  drop?: string | null;   // 破坏掉落 item key（缺省掉同名 key；null = 显式无掉落,原版装饰类）
  rope?: boolean;        // 绳索类（攀爬判定）
  blend: boolean;        // 是否参与同类边缘融合
  attach?: 'ground' | 'wall'; // 装饰物附着需求
  w?: number; h?: number;     // framed 物体占格数
  vanilla?: {                 // 原版素材渲染（terraria-assets + TEdit 数据）
    sheet: number;            // Tiles_N 表 id
    frame: 'auto' | 'style' | 'blend'; // auto=47 表 8 向；style=显式帧；blend=BlendRules 合并分帧（泥土/石/草族）
    fw?: number; fh?: number; // style 类的占格数（TEdit frameSize）
  };
}

export const T = {
  EMPTY: 0, DIRT: 1, STONE: 2, GRASS: 3,
  ORE_COPPER: 4, ORE_IRON: 5, ORE_SILVER: 6, ORE_GOLD: 7,
  TREE: 8, LEAVES: 9, WOOD: 10, PLATFORM: 11,
  TORCH: 12, WORKBENCH: 13, FURNACE: 14, ANVIL: 15,
  CHEST: 16, DOOR_CLOSED: 17, DOOR_OPEN: 18,
  MUSHROOM: 19, FLOWER: 20, TALLGRASS: 21, SAND: 22, SNOW: 23,
  SAPLING: 24, ASH: 25,
} as const;

  const id = TILE_DEFS.length;
  TILE_DEFS.push({

    opacity: 0, pick: -1, axe: -1, mapColor: '#000', blend: false, ...d, id,
  } as TileDef);
  return id;
}

1	// 设置项单例 + KvStore 持久化（M6）。变更通过监听器下发到 Audio/Sfx/UIScale。
2	import { kvGet, kvSet } from '../save/KvStore';
3	import { DEFAULT_LANG, normalizeLang } from '../i18n/GameCulture';
4	
5	export interface OptionsData {
6	  musicVol: number;   // 0..1
7	  sfxVol: number;     // 0..1
8	  uiScale: number;    // 0.75..1.5（作用于 UIScale.userScale）
9	  devMode: boolean;
10	  lang: string;       // culture 名(如 zh-Hans);兼容旧数字 legacyId(对齐原版 config.json)
11	}
12	
13	const KEY = 'sandboxworld.options';
14	
15	export class OptionsStore {
16	  data: OptionsData = {
17	    musicVol: 0.35,
18	    sfxVol: 1,
19	    uiScale: 1,
20	    devMode: false,
21	    lang: DEFAULT_LANG,
22	  };
23	  loaded = false;
24	  private listeners: Array<(d: OptionsData) => void> = [];
25	
26	  onChange(fn: (d: OptionsData) => void) {
27	    this.listeners.push(fn);
28	    if (this.loaded) fn(this.data);
29	  }
30	
31	  async load() {
32	    if (this.loaded) return;
33	    this.loaded = true;
34	    try {
35	      const raw = await kvGet(KEY);
36	      if (raw) Object.assign(this.data, JSON.parse(raw));
37	      // 语言归一化:旧数字 legacyId → culture 名;非法值落 en-US(对齐原版 LoadSettings)
38	      this.data.lang = normalizeLang(this.data.lang);
39	    } catch { /* 无存档用默认 */ }
40	    this.emit();
41	  }
42	
43	  /** 改值并持久化（局部更新） */
44	  async set<K extends keyof OptionsData>(key: K, value: OptionsData[K]) {
45	    this.data[key] = value;
46	    this.emit();
47	    try {
48	      await kvSet(KEY, JSON.stringify(this.data));
49	    } catch { /* 存储不可用则仅内存生效 */ }
50	  }
51	
52	  private emit() {
53	    for (const fn of this.listeners) fn(this.data);
54	  }
55	}
56	
57	export const options = new OptionsStore();
58

397	    swing: { t: number; dur: number; item: number } | null,
398	    hover: { x: number; y: number } | null,
399	    boss: { name: string; hp: number; maxHp: number; cx: number; cy: number } | null,
400	    mouseX = 0, mouseY = 0, mouseDown = false,
401	    mineProgress = 0,
402	    flicker: FlickerClock | null = null,
403	    remotePlayers: Player[] = [],
404	    invasion: { name: string; pct: number } | null = null,
405	  ) {
406	    this._mouseX = mouseX;
407	    this.remotePlayers = remotePlayers;
408	    this._mouseY = mouseY;
409	    this._mouseDown = mouseDown;
410	    this._liquidNow = performance.now(); // 帧 first thing 采样：背景水/瀑布/前景水共用同一时刻
411	    this.updateWaterStyle(world, cam);
412	    this.easeFullMap();
413	    const ctx = this.ctx;
414	    const viewW = this.canvas.width, viewH = this.canvas.height;
415	    cam.viewW = viewW; cam.viewH = viewH;
416	    const z = cam.zoom;
417	
418	    // 1. 天空
419	    this.sky.draw(ctx, clock, viewW, viewH, cam.x);
420	
421	    // 1.5 世界背景图（原版 DrawBG：地表群系视差层 + 地下分层；天空之后、tile 之前）
422	    if (this.scene) {
423	      // 昼夜色调（≈ColorOfTheSkies：白天白、夜晚压暗偏蓝、晨昏暖色）
424	      const df = clock.dayFactor;
425	      const t = clock.timeOfDay;
426	      let tr = 1, tg = 1, tb = 1;
427	      if (df < 1) {
428	        const dusk = t > 0.68 && t < 0.82 ? 1 - Math.abs(t - 0.75) / 0.07 : t > 0.18 && t < 0.32 ? 1 - Math.abs(t - 0.25) / 0.07 : 0;
429	        const night = 1 - df;
430	        tr = 1 - 0.70 * night + 0.10 * dusk;
431	        tg = 1 - 0.66 * night - 0.22 * dusk;
432	        tb = 1 - 0.50 * night - 0.40 * dusk;
433	      }
434	      this.biomeBg.draw(ctx, world, this.scene, cam, viewW, viewH, [tr, tg, tb], 16.7);
435	    }
436	
437	    ctx.save();
438	    // 世界变换：平移 + 缩放（以屏幕中心为相机中心）
439	    // 像素风关键：tile/实体用最近邻采样保持锐利（光照层单独用平滑）
440	    // ★像素吸附：相机坐标吸附到 1/zoom 网格——整数世界坐标(全部 chunk/tile 绘制位)
441	    // 变换后精确落在整数 canvas 像素。浮点相机 + 非整数 zoom(默认 1.25)下,相邻
442	    // chunk 各自独立光栅化会在接缝处产生 1px 缺口(每 256 世界 px 一条发丝缝,
443	    // 2026-08-10 实证);吸附后相机以 z 像素步进,像素风反而更稳
444	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
445	    const camRX = Math.round(cam.x * z) / z;
446	    const camRY = Math.round(cam.y * z) / z;
447	    ctx.translate(Math.round(viewW / 2), Math.round(viewH / 2));
448	    ctx.scale(z, z);
449	    ctx.translate(-camRX, -camRY);
450	
451	    // 2. chunks 绘制序列（对照原版 Main.cs 帧序：背景水 → 墙 → 方块 → 瀑布 → 实体 → 前景水）
452	    const ts = TILE;
453	    const x0 = Math.floor((cam.x - viewW / 2 / z) / (CHUNK * ts)) - 1;
454	    const x1 = Math.floor((cam.x + viewW / 2 / z) / (CHUNK * ts)) + 1;
455	    const y0 = Math.floor((cam.y - viewH / 2 / z) / (CHUNK * ts)) - 1;
456	    const y1 = Math.floor((cam.y + viewH / 2 / z) / (CHUNK * ts)) + 1;
457	    const chunkVisible = (cx: number, cy: number) =>
458	      cx >= 0 && cy >= 0 && cx * CHUNK < world.w && cy * CHUNK < world.h;
459	    // 2a. 液体背景 pass（原版 backWaterTarget 先于墙合成，Main.cs:46619）：
460	    //     不透明水画在墙/方块之前——方块贴图透明像素处露出这层水 = 浸润，
461	    //     有墙的水格由墙盖住、只留前景 0.6 层 → 墙在水中可见
462	    this.drawLiquids(world, cam, viewW, viewH, z, true);
463	
464	    // 2b. 背景墙层(+1 canvas px 保险重叠:相邻 chunk 独立光栅化接缝的兜底,
465	    //      nearest 采样下重叠=复制边缘像素,视觉无感)
466	    const over = 1;
467	    for (let cy = y0; cy <= y1; cy++) {
468	      for (let cx = x0; cx <= x1; cx++) {
469	        if (!chunkVisible(cx, cy)) continue;
470	        ctx.drawImage(chunks.get(cx, cy).wall, cx * CHUNK * ts, cy * CHUNK * ts, CHUNK * ts + over, CHUNK * ts + over);
471	      }
472	    }
473	
474	    // 2c. 前景 tile/物体层(同款保险重叠)

```

// 设置面板（DOM，M6）：分栏 声音/界面/游戏/数据 + 返回。
// 主菜单与游戏内（暂停菜单）共用；数据栏仅主菜单显示。
// 文案全部走 Lang（M8 语言系统）：原版键（LegacyMenu.*）+ 自有键（Mods.SandboxWorld.*）。
// 语言切换：网格按钮（原版语义=用当前语言显示所有语言名，Language.* 类别），
// 点击后 Lang.setLanguage → 本面板经 Lang.onChange 重建（保留当前分栏）。
import { options } from '../core/Options';
import { UIScale } from '../vui/draw/UIScale';
import { UISfx } from '../vui/UISfx';
import { Lang } from '../i18n/Lang';

export interface SettingsFlow {
  /** 主菜单专属：继续存档 / 读档 / 导入 .wld / 开发者模式 */
  onQuickLoad?(): void;
  onLoadFile?(): void;
  onImportWld?(): void;
  onBack(): void;
}

const CSS = `
.sw-set-panel {
  position: fixed; left: 50%; top: 50%; transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
  width: 560px; max-width: 96vw; z-index: 25; cursor: auto;
  background: linear-gradient(160deg, #2b3664, #1c2444);
  border: 2px solid #7d92d6; border-radius: 6px; padding: 14px 16px; color: #e8e8f4;
  font-family: "Fusion Pixel 12px", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.6);
}
.sw-set-title { text-align: center; font-size: 18px; color: #ffe8a0; margin-bottom: 10px;
  text-shadow: 1px 1px 0 #000, -1px -1px 0 #000, 1px -1px 0 #000, -1px 1px 0 #000; }
.sw-set-body { display: flex; gap: 12px; min-height: 260px; }
.sw-set-cats { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; width: 96px; }
.sw-set-cats div {
  padding: 8px 10px; background: #232c52; border: 1px solid #3a4680;
  border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; text-align: center;
}
.sw-set-cats div.active { background: #5a6ac0; color: #fff; outline: 1px solid #ffd76e; }
.sw-set-content { flex: 1; background: #1a2140; border: 1px solid #3a4680;
  border-radius: 4px; padding: 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px;
  overflow-y: auto; max-height: 60vh; }
.sw-set-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
.sw-set-row > span:first-child { width: 88px; color: #c8d0f0; }
.sw-set-row input[type=range] { flex: 1; accent-color: #7d92d6; }
.sw-set-row .val { width: 40px; text-align: right; color: #ffe8a0; }
.sw-set-row input[type=checkbox] { width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: #7d92d6; }
.sw-set-btns { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
.sw-set-btns button, .sw-set-back {
  background: #3a4680; color: #e8e8f4; border: 1px solid #7d92d6; border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 8px 12px; cursor: pointer; font-family: inherit; text-align: left;
}
.sw-set-btns button:hover, .sw-set-back:hover { background: #4a5aa0; }
.sw-set-footer { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; margin-top: 12px; }
.sw-set-back { padding: 6px 22px; }
/* 语言选择网格（M8,视觉对齐 sw-wc-opts 互斥选项模式） */
.sw-set-lang-title { color: #ffe8a0; font-size: 14px; }
.sw-set-lang-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); gap: 6px; }
.sw-set-lang-grid button {
  background: #232c52; color: #e8e8f4; border: 1px solid #3a4680; border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 7px 10px; cursor: pointer; font-family: inherit; text-align: left;
}
.sw-set-lang-grid button:hover { background: #4a5aa0; }

```

Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Interface = 界面
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Gameplay = 游戏
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Data = 数据
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.UiScale = UI 缩放
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.UiScaleHint = 缩放影响角色选择/世界创建等画布界面的尺寸。
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.DevMode = 开发者模式
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.DevModeHint = 开发者模式:下次进入新世界时获得全部道具与图块展示区。
Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FontHint = 提示:非中文语言暂用系统字体渲染。

// UI 贴图语义封装：vanilla-ui 键名 → DrawRect。
// 键 = vanilla-ui-whitelist.json 的源路径下划线展平（UI/PanelBackground.png → UI_PanelBackground）。
import type { DrawRect } from '../../assets/SpriteAtlas';
import type { SpriteAtlas } from '../../assets/SpriteAtlas';

let atlas: SpriteAtlas | null = null;

export const UITextures = {
  setAtlas(a: SpriteAtlas) { atlas = a; },

  /** 按白名单键取贴图（自动补 .png 后缀），缺失返回 null（控件须自兜底） */
  get(name: string): DrawRect | null {
    return atlas?.vui(name) ?? atlas?.vui(`${name}.png`) ?? null;
  },
};

// 挖掘/砍伐击数验证（原版 1:1，阈值 100，冷却 useTime）：
// 伤害公式（Player.cs:45098 axe×1.2 / :52981 pick + 分档除数 / tileNoFail=100）
// 铜斧(7)砍树：int(7×1.2)=8/击 → 13 击（用户实测原版 ≈13，此前我们 ×5.5=3 击是错的）
// 铜镐(35)泥土：35/击 → 3 击；金镐(55)石：55/击 → 2 击；银镐(45)银矿(门槛20)：45→3 击
// tileNoFail（草丛 sheet 3 等）：100 → 1 击
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { ITEM_DEFS, ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/items';
import { TILE_DEFS, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

/** 原版每击伤害（Game.tryMine 移植的同一公式——此处独立复算做回归锚） */
export function vanillaHitDamage(sheet: number, toolType: 'pick' | 'axe', power: number): number {
  const NO_FAIL = new Set([3, 4, 24, 32, 35, 50, 51, 52, 61, 62, 69, 73, 74, 81, 82, 83, 84, 110, 113, 115, 129, 162, 165, 184, 185, 186, 187, 192, 201, 205, 227, 233, 254, 324, 330, 331, 332, 333, 373, 374, 375, 382, 384, 461, 481, 482, 483, 484, 485, 518, 519, 528, 529, 530, 549, 624, 636, 637, 638, 654, 655, 656, 666, 697, 700, 701, 705, 709]);
  const AXE = new Set([5, 72, 80, 323, 488, 583, 584, 585, 586, 587, 589, 596, 616, 634, 704]);
  if (NO_FAIL.has(sheet)) return 100;
  if (AXE.has(sheet)) return toolType === 'axe' ? Math.floor((sheet === 80 ? power * 3 : power) * 1.2) : 0;
  if (toolType !== 'pick') return 0;
  let dmg = power;
  if (sheet === 107 || sheet === 221) dmg = Math.floor(power / 2);
  else if (sheet === 108 || sheet === 222) dmg = Math.floor(power / 3);
  else if (sheet === 111 || sheet === 223) dmg = Math.floor(power / 4);
  else if (sheet === 211) dmg = Math.floor(power / 5);
  else if (sheet === 226 || sheet === 237) dmg = Math.floor(power / 4);
  else if ([25, 203, 117, 58, 41, 43, 44, 481, 482, 483].includes(sheet)) dmg = Math.floor(power / 2);
  else if (sheet === 48 || sheet === 232) dmg = power * 2;
  // 门槛（取地表上方；地下门槛建另一用例）
  const gates: Array<[number, number]> = [[211, 200], [25, 65], [203, 65], [117, 65], [37, 50], [56, 55], [58, 65], [226, 210], [237, 210], [41, 100], [43, 100], [44, 100]];
  for (const [sh, gate] of gates) if (sheet === sh && power < gate) return 0;
  return dmg;
}

const hits = (sheet: number, toolType: 'pick' | 'axe', power: number): number => {
  const d = vanillaHitDamage(sheet, toolType, power);
  if (d <= 0) return Infinity;
  return Math.ceil(100 / d);
};

describe('工具数据（原版 Item.cs SetDefaults 提取值核对）', () => {
  it('铜镐/铜斧/铁镐/铁斧/银/金 全字段', () => {
    const expect_ = (key: string, type: string, power: number, damage: number, useTime: number) => {
      const t = ITEM_DEFS[ITEM_BY_KEY[key]]?.tool;
      expect(t?.type).toBe(type);
      expect(t?.power).toBe(power);
      expect(t?.damage).toBe(damage);
      expect(t?.useTime).toBe(useTime);
    };
    expect_('copper_pickaxe', 'pick', 35, 4, 15);   // 3509
    expect_('copper_axe', 'axe', 7, 3, 21);          // 3506
    expect_('iron_pickaxe', 'pick', 40, 5, 13);       // 1
    expect_('iron_axe', 'axe', 9, 5, 19);             // 10
    expect_('silver_pickaxe', 'pick', 45, 6, 11);     // 3515
    expect_('silver_axe', 'axe', 10, 6, 18);          // 3512
    expect_('gold_pickaxe', 'pick', 55, 6, 17);       // 3521
    expect_('gold_axe', 'axe', 11, 7, 18);            // 3518
    expect_('wood_hammer', 'hammer', 25, 2, 25);      // 196
  });
});

describe('击数（阈值 100，原版公式）', () => {
  it('树：斧族专属；铜斧 13 击 / 铁斧 10 击 / 金斧 8 击', () => {
    expect(hits(5, 'axe', 7)).toBe(13);    // int(8.4)=8 → ceil(100/8)=13

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { torchAnchorFrame, torchStillAnchored } from '../src/world/Torch';
import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

// 火把锚定（TileObjectData tile4 + TileObject.CanPlace 1:1）
function mkStore(w = 8, h = 8): TileStore {
  const st = new TileStore(w, h);
  return st;
}

describe('火把锚定（torchAnchorFrame）', () => {
  it('底锚优先：下方实心 → frameX 0（TileObject base 先于 alternates）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(4, 4, T.STONE);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('左锚：无地面但左邻实心 → frameX 22（AnchorLeft）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(3, 3, T.STONE);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(22);
  });

  it('右锚：仅右邻实心 → frameX 44（AnchorRight）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(5, 3, T.STONE);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(44);
  });

  it('墙锚：无任何方块支撑但有背景墙 → frameX 0（AnchorWall，直立贴墙）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setWallSilent(4, 3, TILE_BY_KEY['stone'] ?? 1);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('无锚点 → -1（放置拒绝）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(-1);
  });

  it('支撑消失掉落（torchStillAnchored）：挖掉地板后直立火把掉、有墙仍留', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(4, 4, T.STONE);
    st.setTile(4, 3, T.TORCH, 0, 0);
    expect(torchStillAnchored(st, 4, 3, 0)).toBe(true);
    st.setTile(4, 4, 0); // 挖掉地板
    expect(torchStillAnchored(st, 4, 3, 0)).toBe(false);
    st.setWallSilent(4, 3, 1); // 贴墙 → 留住
    expect(torchStillAnchored(st, 4, 3, 0)).toBe(true);

  /** 电路工具使用(原版 Player.cs:30289-30444 ItemCheck_UseWiringTools) */
  private useWireTool(tx: number, ty: number, itemDef: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]) {
    if (!this.netCanEditTile()) { this.netDeny(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.RoomProtectTiles')); return; }
    const w = this.wiring;
    if (!w) return;
    const st = this.world.store;
    const dx = tx - Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE);
    const dy = ty - Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty, 20)) return; // 原版多彩扳手/蓝图 tileBoost=20
    if (this.player.useTime > 0) return;
    const wt = itemDef.wireTool!;
    const inv = this.player.inv;

    // 致动魔杖:手动翻转(useTime 防连按)
    if (wt.rod) {
      if (w.actuate(tx, ty)) {
        this.player.useTime = 15;
        this.sfx.play('tink');
      }
      return;
    }
    // 剪线钳:按优先级移除一件(致动器>黄>绿>蓝>红)
    if (wt.cutter) {
      const done =
        w.killActuator(tx, ty) ||
        w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW) ||
        w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN) ||
        w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE) ||
        w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_RED);
      if (done) { this.player.useTime = 5; this.sfx.play('dig'); }
      return;
    }
    // 宏伟蓝图:按下记锚点,松开执行 L 形批量
    if (wt.grand) {
      if (!this.grandAnchor) { this.grandAnchor = [tx, ty]; return; }
      const a = this.grandAnchor;
      this.grandAnchor = null;
      const mode = this.wireToolMode;
      const wires = mode & TOOL_CUTTER ? 9999 : inv.countOf(ITEM_BY_KEY['wire'] ?? -1);
      const actuators = mode & TOOL_CUTTER ? 9999 : inv.countOf(ITEM_BY_KEY['actuator_item'] ?? -1);
      const res = w.massWireOperation(a, [tx, ty], mode, wires, actuators);
      if (!(mode & TOOL_CUTTER)) {
        if (res.wire > 0) inv.remove(ITEM_BY_KEY['wire'] ?? -1, res.wire);
        if (res.actuator > 0) inv.remove(ITEM_BY_KEY['actuator_item'] ?? -1, res.actuator);
      }
      this.player.useTime = 10;
      this.sfx.play('place');
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      return;
    }
    // 五彩扳手:单点,用当前模式
    const mode = itemDef.key === 'multicolor_wrench' ? this.wireToolMode : (wt.place ?? 0);
    if (mode & TOOL_CUTTER) {
      const done =
        (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR ? w.killActuator(tx, ty) : false) ||
        (mode & TOOL_YELLOW ? w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW) : false) ||
        (mode & TOOL_GREEN ? w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN) : false) ||
        (mode & TOOL_BLUE ? w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE) : false) ||
        (mode & TOOL_RED ? w.killWire(tx, ty, TileStore.WIRE_RED) : false);
      if (done) { this.player.useTime = 5; this.sfx.play('dig'); }
      return;
    }
    // 扳手/致动器放置
    let needWire = 0, needActuator = 0;
    const colorBits: Array<[number, number]> = [
      [TOOL_RED, TileStore.WIRE_RED], [TOOL_GREEN, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN],
      [TOOL_BLUE, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE], [TOOL_YELLOW, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW],
    ];
    for (const [m, bit] of colorBits) if (mode & m && !(st.wire[st.idx(tx, ty)] & bit)) needWire++;
    if (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR && !(st.wire[st.idx(tx, ty)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR)) needActuator++;
    const wireHave = inv.countOf(ITEM_BY_KEY['wire'] ?? -1);
    const actHave = inv.countOf(ITEM_BY_KEY['actuator_item'] ?? -1);
    if (needWire > wireHave || needActuator > actHave) {
      this.cb.onToast(needActuator > actHave ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NoActuator') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NoWire'));
      return;
    }
    let placed = false;
    for (const [m, bit] of colorBits) {
      if (mode & m && w.placeWire(tx, ty, bit)) placed = true;
    }
    if (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR && w.placeActuator(tx, ty)) placed = true;
    if (placed) {
      if (needWire > 0) inv.remove(ITEM_BY_KEY['wire'] ?? -1, needWire);
      if (needActuator > 0) inv.remove(ITEM_BY_KEY['actuator_item'] ?? -1, needActuator);
      this.player.useTime = 5;
      this.sfx.play('place');
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    }
    void dy;
  }

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------

```

	public static bool GemTreeGroundTest(int tileType)
	{
		if (tileType < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (TileID.Sets.Conversion.Stone[tileType] || TileID.Sets.Conversion.Moss[tileType])
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool VanityTreeGroundTest(int tileType)
	{
		if (tileType < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (TileID.Sets.Conversion.Grass[tileType] && tileType != 23 && tileType != 199)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

```
	public static bool HitLine(int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, int ignoreX, int ignoreY, List<Point> ignoreTargets, out Point col)
	{
		int value = x1;
		int value2 = y1;
		int value3 = x2;
		int value4 = y2;
		value = Utils.Clamp(value, 1, Main.maxTilesX - 1);
		value3 = Utils.Clamp(value3, 1, Main.maxTilesX - 1);
		value2 = Utils.Clamp(value2, 1, Main.maxTilesY - 40);
		value4 = Utils.Clamp(value4, 1, Main.maxTilesY - 40);
		float num = Math.Abs(value - value3);
		float num2 = Math.Abs(value2 - value4);
		if (num == 0f && num2 == 0f)
		{
			col = new Point(value, value2);

If col==endpoint, first check skips, second adds if endpoint is solid.)

So net: targets = for each (start,end) pair: run DDA; if blocked at cell c (c != end) → add c; if end solid → add end. Simplify: run hitLine; if result hit cell h: add h if h is solid&&!platform or h != end.

So all components are -1/0/1 effectively. E.g. diagonal (0.707,0.707)*1.5=(1.06,1.06)→(1,1). Cardinal (1,0)*1.5=(1.5,0)→(1,0). Good: standard 8-dir.

Implementation: angle = atan2(y,x); quantize to 45°; dir = (round(cos*1.5), round(sin*1.5))? ToPoint() in XNA = (int)x truncation toward zero. For 1.5 → 1. For -1.5 → -1. So use Math.trunc. cos45*1.5=1.0606→1. OK I'll compute via the same formula to be safe:
```
const rot = Math.atan2(dy, dx); let a = rot; if (a < 0) a += 2π;
const step = π/4;
const idx = Math.floor(((a + step/2) % (2π)) / step);
const ang = idx * step;
const v = { x: Math.cos(ang)*1.5, y: Math.sin(ang)*1.5 };
return { x: Math.trunc(v.x), y: Math.trunc(v.y) };
```

	public static bool EmptyTile(int i, int j, bool ignoreTiles = false)
	{
		Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(i * 16, j * 16, 16, 16);
		if (Main.tile[i, j].active() && !ignoreTiles)
		{
			return false;
		}
		for (int k = 0; k < 255; k++)
		{
			if (Main.player[k].active && !Main.player[k].dead && !Main.player[k].ghost && rectangle.Intersects(new Rectangle((int)Main.player[k].position.X, (int)Main.player[k].position.Y, Main.player[k].width, Main.player[k].height)))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		for (int l = 0; l < Main.maxNPCs; l++)
		{
			if (Main.npc[l].active && rectangle.Intersects(new Rectangle((int)Main.npc[l].position.X, (int)Main.npc[l].position.Y, Main.npc[l].width, Main.npc[l].height)))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

    // Main.tileAxe\n/** WorldGen.CanKillTile 子集（SmartCursorHelper 用法：不查镐力，只看\"不可破坏\"） */\nconst canKillTile = (t: number): boolean => (TILE_DEFS[t]?.pick ?? -1) >= 0;\nconst tileFrameImportantType = (t: number): boolean => !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.framed;  // Main.tileFrameImportant\nconst tileCutType = (t: number): boolean => !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.decor;              // Main.tileCut（可割植物）\n\nconst activeAt = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean => st.isActive(x, y);\nconst inActiveAt = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean => st.isActuated(x, y);\n/** Main.tileNoAttach 无独立数据源：实心即认为可附着（近似，原版仅少数方块 noAttach） */\nconst tileNoAttachType = (_t: number): boolean => false;\n\n/** WorldGen.SolidTile（WorldGen.cs SolidTile(Tile)）:nactive && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && !half && slope==0 */\nfunction solidTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  if (!activeAt(st, x, y) || tileSolidTopType(st.type[i])) return false;\n  return tileSolidType(st.type[i]) && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0;\n}\n\n/** WorldGen.SolidTile2（Acorns/GemCorns 用）:active && tileSolid && slope==0 && !half && !inActive */\nfunction solidTile2(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  if (!activeAt(st, x, y)) return false;\n  return tileSolidType(st.type[i]) && st.slope[i] === 0 && st.half[i] === 0;\n}\n\n/** Collision.EmptyTile(ignoreTiles:true)（Collision.cs:1361）:无玩家/NPC 与该格相交 */\nfunction emptyTile(env: SmartCursorEnv, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  const rx = x * 16, ry = y * 16;\n  for (const o of env.occupants) {\n    if (o.x < rx + 16 && o.x + o.w > rx && o.y < ry + 16 && o.y + o.h > ry) return false;\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** WorldGen.EmptyTileCheck：矩形内全部为空，或恰为 ignoreType 的树苗 */\nfunction emptyTileCheck(st: TileStore, x1: number, x2: number, y1: number, y2: number, ignoreType: number): boolean {\n  for (let x = x1; x <= x2; x++) {\n    for (let y = y1; y <= y2; y++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      if (st.type[i] !== 0 && sheetOf(st.type[i]) !== ignoreType) return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** Collision.InTileBounds */\nfunction inTileBounds(x: number, y: number, x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number): boolean {\n  return x >= x0 && x <= x1 && y >= y0 && y <= y1;\n}\n\nconst toWorld = (p: Pt): { x: number; y: number } => ({ x: p.x * 16 + 8, y: p.y * 16 + 8 });\nconst dist = (ax: number, ay: number, bx: number, by: number): number => Math.hypot(ax - bx, ay - by);\n\n/** 扫描型策略公共收尾：取离鼠标最近的候选（SmartCursorHelper 各策略统一模式） */\nfunction nearestOf(targets: Pt[], mouse: { x: number; y: number }): Pt | null {\n  if (targets.length === 0) return null;\n  let best = -1;\n  let point = targets[0];\n  for (const t of targets) {\n    const w = toWorld(t);\n    const d = dist(w.x, w.y, mouse.x, mouse.y);\n    if (best === -1 || d < best) { best = d; point = t; }\n  }\n  return point;\n}\n\n// ===================== Collision.HitLine 1:1（Collision.cs:782-979） =====================\n\nexport interface HitLineResult { hit: boolean; col: Pt }\n\n/**\n * 镐射线：DDA 从 (x1,y1) 走到 (x2,y2)，返回首个 `tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && !inActive` 的格。\n * ignoreX/ignoreY 是 MineSolids 传入的 `signX*gravDir / -signY*gravDir`（起点一侧的邻居不判）。\n * 返回 hit=false 表示线路上出现越界格；hit=true 且 col==终点表示一路畅通。\n */\nexport function hitLine(\n  st: TileStore, x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number,\n  ignoreX: number, ignoreY: number, ignoreTargets: ReadonlyArray<Pt>,\n): HitLineResult {\n  let value = Math.max(1, Math.min(x1, st.w - 1));\n  let value2 = Math.max(1, Math.min(y1, Math.max(2, st.h - 40)));\n  let value3 = Math.max(1, Math.min(x2, st.w - 1));\n  let value4 = Math.max(1, Math.min(y2, Math.max(2, st.h - 40)));\n  const blocked = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n    const i = st.idx(x, y);\n    return !inActiveAt(st, x, y) && activeAt(st, x, y)\n      && tileSolidType(st.type[i]) && !tileSolidTopType(st.type[i]);\n  };\n  const ignored = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>\n    ignoreTargets.some((t) => t.x === x && t.y === y);\n  const num = Math.abs(value - value3);\n  const num2 = Math.abs(value2 - value4);\n  if (num === 0 && num2 === 0) return { hit: true, col: { x: value, y: value2 } };\n  let num3 = 1, num4 = 1;\n  if (num === 0 || num2 === 0) {\n    if (num === 0) num3 = 0;\n    if (num2 === 0) num4 = 0;\n  } else if (num > num2) num3 = num / num2;\n  else num4 = num2 / num;\n  let num5 = 0, num6 = 0;\n  let num7 = value2 < value4 ? 2 : 1;\n  let num8 = Math.floor(num), num9 = Math.floor(num2);\n  const num10 = Math.sign(value3 - value);\n  const num11 = Math.sign(value4 - value2);\n  let flag = false, flag2 = false;\n  do {\n    if (num7 === 2) {\n      // 纵向步进（先走 Y）\n      num5 += num3;\n      let num13 = Math.floor(num5);\n      num5 -= num13;\n      for (let j = 0; j < num13; j++) {\n        if (!ignored(value, value2) && !ignored(value, value2 - 1) && !ignored(value, value2 + 1)) {\n          if (ignoreY !== -1 && num11 < 0 && blocked(value, value2 - 1)) return { hit: true, col: { x: value, y: value2 - 1 } };\n          if (ignoreY !== 1 && num11 > 0 && blocked(value, value2 + 1)) return { hit: true, col: { x: value, y: value2 + 1 } };\n          if (blocked(value, value2)) return { hit: true, col: { x: value, y: value2 } };\n        }\n        if (num8 === 0 && num9 === 0) { flag = true; Number(m[1]) : null;\n}\n\ninterface HeldSemantics {\n  vid: number | null;\n  pick: number; axe: number; hammer: number;\n  createTileSheet: number | null;\n  createWall: number | null;\n  placeStyle: number;\n}\n\nfunction heldSemantics(held: SmartCursorHeld): HeldSemantics {\n  const def = held.def;\n  const tool = held.tool;\n  return {\n    vid: vanillaItemIdOf(def),\n    pick: tool?.type === 'pick' ? tool.power : 0,\n    axe: tool?.type === 'axe' ? tool.power : (tool?.type === 'hammer' ? i <= reach.x1; i++) {\n      for (let j = reach.y0; j <= reach.y1; j++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;\n        const t = st.get(i, j);\n        const below = st.get(i, j + 1);\n        // :496-497 自身可替换（可破坏放置/可割非药草/已成熟可收）+ 下方 nactive 平顶\n        const replaceable = !activeAt(st, i, j)\n          || tileCutType(t) && sheetOf(t) !== 82 && sheetOf(t) !== 83;\n        if (!replaceable) continue;\n        if (!activeAt(st, i, j + 1) || st.half[st.idx(i, j + 1)] !== 0 || st.slope[st.idx(i, j + 1)] !== 0) continue;\n        const belowSheet = sheetOf(below);\n        const liq = st.liquid[st.idx(i, j)];\n        // :502-546 各样式（草药）适配基质\n        const ok = (() => {\n          switch (style) {\n            case 0: return [78, 380, 2, 477, 109, 492].includes(belowSheet) && liq === 0;\n            case 1: return [78, 380, 60].includes(belowSheet) && liq === 0;\n            case 2: return [78, 380, 0, 59].includes(belowSheet) && liq === 0;\n            case 3: return [78, 380, 203, 199, 23, 25].includes(belowSheet) && liq === 0;\n            case 4: return [78, 380, 53, 116].includes(belowSheet) && (liq === 0 || liq > 0);\n            case 5: return [78, 380, 57, 633].includes(belowSheet);\n            case 6: return [78, 380, 147, 161, 163, 164, 200].includes(belowSheet);\n            default: return false;\n          }\n        })();\n        if (ok) targets.push({ x: i, y: j });\n      }\n    }\n    const point = nearestOf(targets, env.mouse);\n    if (point && inTileBounds(point.x, point.y, reach.x0, reach.y0, reach.x1, reach.y1)) set(point.x, point.y);\n  }\n\n  /** :427-480 种植盆（createTile 380）：已有盆的左右空位 */\n  private stepPlanterBox(\n    env: SmartCursorEnv, sem: HeldSemantics, reach: Region,\n    set: (x: number, y: number) => void, taken: () => boolean,\n  ): void {\n    if (sem.createTileSheet !== 380 || taken()) return;\n    const st = env.st;\n    const targets: Pt[] = [];\n    for (let i = reach.x0; i <= reach.x1; i++) {\n      for (let j = reach.y0; j <= reach.y1; j++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(i, j) || sheetOf(st.get(i, j)) !== 380) continue;\n        // :448-455 盆左右为空/可替换 → 续放\n        for (const ox of [-1, 1]) {\n          const nx = i + ox;\n          if (!st.inBounds(nx, j)) continue;\n          const nt = st.get(nx, j);\n          if (!activeAt(st, nx, j) || tileCutType(nt)) targets.push({ x: nx, y: j });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    const point = nearestOf(targets, env.mouse);\n    if (point && inTileBounds(point.x, point.y, reach.x0, reach.y0, reach.x1, reach.y1)) set(point.x, point.y);\n  }\n\n  /** :371-425 陶盆（createTile 78）：空位 + 下方平实心顶 */\n  private stepClayPots(\n    env: SmartCursorEnv, sem: HeldSemantics, reach: Region, screenX: number, screenY: number,\n    set: (x: number, y: number) => void, taken: () => boolean,\n  ): void {\n    if (sem.createTileSheet !== 78 || taken()) return;\n    const st = env.st;\n    // :378-386 鼠标格占用/不可达 → 停用\n    if (activeAt(st, screenX, screenY)) return;\n    if (!inTileBounds(screenX, screenY, reach.x0, reach.y0, reach.x1, reach.y1)) return;\n    const targets: Pt[] = [];\n    for (let i = reach.x0; i <= reach.x1; i++) {\n      for (let j = reach.y0; j <= reach.y1; j++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;\n        const t = st.get(i, j);\n        const below = st.get(i, j + 1);\n        // :395 自身空/可割，下方 nactive 无坡无半砖且实心\n        if ((!activeAt(st, i, j) || tileCutType(t))\n          && activeAt(st, i, j + 1) && st.half[st.idx(i, j + 1)] === 0 && st.slope[st.idx(i, j + 1)] === 0\n          && tileSolidType(below)) {\n          targets.push({ x: i, y: j });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    const open = targets.filter((p) => emptyTile(env, p.x, p.y));\n    const point = nearestOf(open, env.mouse);\n    if (point && inTileBounds(point.x, point.y, reach.x0, reach.y0, reach.x1, reach.y1)) set(point.x, point.y);\n  }\n\n  /** :214-254 再生法杖（213/5295）：暴露泥土格取最近 */\n  private stepStaffOfRegrowth(\n    env: SmartCursorEnv, sem: HeldSemantics, reach: Region,\n    set: (x: number, y: number) => void, taken: () => boolean,\n  ): void {\n    if (sem.vid !== 213 && sem.vid !== 5295 || taken()) return;\n    const st = env.st;\n    const targets: Pt[] = [];\n    for (let i = reach.x0; i <= reach.x1; i++) {\n      for (let j = reach.y0; j <= reach.y1; j++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;\n        // :228 泥土(0) 且暴露（四邻或四角有空）\n        if (activeAt(st, i, j) && !inActiveAt(st, i, j) && sheetOf(st.get(i, j)) === 0 && exposed8(st, i, j)) {\n          targets.push({ x: i, y: j });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    const point = nearestOf(targets, env.mouse);\n    if (point && inTileBounds(point.x, point.y, reach.x0, reach.y0, reach.x1, reach.y1)) set(point.x, point.y);\n  }\n}\n\n// ===================== 模块级工具 =====================\n\ninterface Region { x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number }\n\n/** TileReachCheckSettings.GetTileRegion（TileReachCheckSettings.cs:53）+ clamp 世界 [10,max-10]（:100-103） */\nfunction tileRegion(p: SmartCursorActor, tb: number, st: TileStore): Region {\n  const x = Math.min(20, 5) + tb;\n  const y = Math.min(20, 3) + tb;\n  const LX = Math.floor(p.x / 16) - x;\n  const HX = Math.ceil((p.x + p.w) / 16) - 1 + x;\n  const LY = Math.floor(p.y / 16) - y;\n  const HY = Math.ceil((p.y + p.h) / 16) - 1 + y;\n  return {\n    x0: Math.max(10, Math.min(LX, st.w - 10)),\n    x1: Math.max(10, Math.min(HX, st.w - 10)),\n    y0: Math.max(10, Math.min(LY, st.h - 10)),\n    y1: Math.max(10, Math.min(HY, st.h - 10)),\n  };\n}\n\n/** :183-199 让位集（DisableSmartCursor / 轨道 314 且 gravDir=1） */\nfunction isHoveringOverAnInteractableTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !activeAt(st, x, y)) return false;\n  const sheet = sheetOf(st.get(x, y));\n  if (DISABLE_SMART_CURSOR_SHEETS.has(sheet)) return true;\n  if (sheet === 314) return true; // gravDir 恒 1（反重力未实装）\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** Main.tileHammer 子集：可被锤的方块（实心非平台即可锤整形） */\nfunction isHammerTile(sheet: number): boolean {\n  return sheet >= 0;\n}\n\n/** Step_Platforms 辅助：格上是平台族 sheet（:1445-1452） */\nfunction isPlatformSheet(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !activeAt(st, x, y)) return false;\n  return PLATFORMS.has(sheetOf(st.get(x, y)));\n}\n\n/** :201-212 AllowNormalBlockPlacementBehaviourForItemType（草种/苔藓物品不参与方块策略） */\nfunction allowNormalBlockPlacement(sem: HeldSemantics): boolean {\n  const vid = sem.vid;\n  if (vid == null) return true;\n  return !(vid === 213 || vid === 5295 || GRASS_SEED_ITEMS.has(vid) || MOSS_ITEMS.has(vid));\n}\n\n/** 放置物为实心非平台非框架方块（BlocksFilling/Lines 门 :812/:2709） */\nfunction isSolidBlockSheet(sheet: number): boolean {\n  const tileId = sheetToTileId(sheet);\n  if (tileId == null) return false;\n  const d = TILE_DEFS[tileId];\n  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !d.framed;\n}\n\nfunction sheetToTileId(sheet: number): number | null {\n  if (sheet < 0) return null;\n  const hit = TILE_DEFS.findIndex((d) => d?.vanilla?.sheet === sheet);\n  return hit >= 0 ? hit : null;\n}\n\n/** 八向至少一格暴露（GrassSeeds/Moss/StaffOfRegrowth 共用 :273-274） */\nfunction exposed8(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  for (const [ox, oy] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]]) {\n    const nx = x + ox, ny = y + oy;\n    if (st.inBounds(nx, ny) && !activeAt(st, nx, ny)) return true;\n  }\n  for (const [ox, oy] of [[-1, -1], [-1, 1], [1, 1], [1, -1]]) {\n    const nx = x + ox, ny = y + oy;\n    if (st.inBounds(nx, ny) && !activeAt(st, nx, ny)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** :2867-2893 IsValidSpotForTorch（墙 → 左 → 右 → 下） */\nfunction isValidSpotForTorch(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const ii = st.idx(x, y);\n  if (st.wall[ii] > 0) return true; // :2869 有墙即放\n  if (TORCH_TILES.has(sheetOf(st.type[ii]))) return false; // :2873 自身是火把\n  const isTreeType = (tx: number, ty: number): boolean => {\n    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty) || !activeAt(st, tx, ty)) return false;\n    return IS_A_TREE_TRUNK.has(sheetOf(st.get(tx, ty)));\n  };\n  // :2877-2881 左：slope 允许（0 或奇数坡）且 实心/梁/连续三格树干\n  if (st.inBounds(x - 1, y) && activeAt(st, x - 1, y)) {\n    const li = st.idx(x - 1, y);\n    const lt = st.get(x - 1, y);\n    const ls = sheetOf(lt);\n    if ((st.slope[li] === 0 || st.slope[li] % 2 !== 1)\n      && ((tileSolidType(lt) && !tileNoAttachType(ls) && !tileSolidTopType(lt) && !NOT_REALLY_SOLID.has(ls))\n        || IS_BEAM.has(ls)\n        || (isTreeType(x - 1, y) && isTreeType(x - 1, y - 1) && isTreeType(x - 1, y + 1)))) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  // :2882-2886 右：slope 允许（0 或偶数坡）\n  if (st.inBounds(x + 1, y) && activeAt(st, x + 1, y)) {\n    const ri = st.idx(x + 1, y);\n    const rt = st.get(x + 1, y);\n    const rs = sheetOf(rt);\n    if ((st.slope[ri] === 0 || st.slope[ri] % 2 !== 0)\n      && ((tileSolidType(rt) && !tileNoAttachType(rs) && !tileSolidTopType(rt) && !NOT_REALLY_SOLID.has(rs))\n        || IS_BEAM.has(rs)\n        || (isTreeType(x + 1, y) && isTreeType(x + 1, y - 1) && isTreeType(x + 1, y + 1)))) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  // :2887-2891 下：slope 0 且非半砖，实心（非平台）或平台\n  if (st.inBounds(x, y + 1) && activeAt(st, x + 0, y + 1)) {\n    const bi = st.idx(x, y + 1);\n    const bt = st.get(x, y + 1);\n    const bs = sheetOf(bt);\n    if (st.slope[bi] === 0 && st.half[bi] === 0\n      && ((tileSolidType(bt) && !tileSolidTopType(bt)) || PLATFORMS.has(bs))\n      && !NOT_REALLY_SOLID.has(bs)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** Collision.HitLineWall 退化：沿线找首个\"有墙或实心\"格（锤射线用） */\nfunction hitLineWall(st: TileStore, x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number): Pt {\n  const steps = Math.max(Math.abs(x2 - x1), Math.abs(y2 - y1));\n  for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {\n    const x = Math.round(x1 + ((x2 - x1) * s) / Math.max(1, steps));\n    const y = Math.round(y1 + ((y2 - y1) * s) / Math.max(1, steps));\n    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) break;\n    const i = st.idx(x, y);\n    if (st.wall[i] > 0 || (activeAt(st, x, y) && tileSolidType(st.type[i]))) return { x, y };\n  }\n  return { x: -1, y: -1 };\n}\n\n/** Collision.HitWallSubstep：该格的墙\"还有得锤\"（至少一邻无墙/无实心） */\nfunction hitWallSubstep(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const w = st.wall[st.idx(x, y)];\n  if (w === 0) return false;\n  for (const [ox, oy] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]] as const) {\n    const nx = x + ox, ny = y + oy;\n    if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) return true;\n    const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);\n    if (st.wall[ni] === 0 && !activeAt(st, nx, ny)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** 锤目标剔除祭坛（26）：SmartCursorHelper.cs:1662/:1704 */\nfunction pickNonAltar(st: TileStore, targets: Pt[], world: (p: Pt) => { x: number; y: number }, ref: { x: number; y: number }): Pt | null {\n  let best = -1;\n  let point: Pt | null = null;\n  for (const t of targets) {\n    if (!st.inBounds(t.x, t.y) || sheetOf(st.get(t.x, t.y)) === 26) continue;\n    const w = world(t);\n    const d = dist(w.x, w.y, ref.x, ref.y);\n    if (best === -1 || d < best) { best = d; point = t; }\n  }\n  return point;\n}\n\n/** CanHitWithCheck 退化（铺墙视线判定）：近似为\"玩家与目标格之间无整实心行/列阻挡\" */\nfunction canSeeTile(st: TileStore, p: SmartCursorActor, tx: number, ty: number): boolean {\n  const x0 = Math.floor(p.x / 16), x1 = Math.floor((p.x + p.w) / 16);\n  const y0 = Math.floor(p.y / 16), y1 = Math.floor((p.y + p.h) / 16);\n  for (let x = Math.min(x0, tx); x <= Math.max(x1, tx); x++) {\n    for (let y = Math.min(y0, ty); y <= Math.max(y1, ty); y++) {\n      if (x >= Math.min(x0, tx) && x <= Math.max(x1, tx) && y >= Math.min(y0, ty) && y <= Math.max(y1, ty)) {\n        // 跳过玩家自身占据区与目标格\n        if (x >= x0 && x <= x1 && y >= y0 && y <= y1) continue;\n        if (x === tx && y === ty) continue;\n      }\n      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;\n      const t = st.get(x, y);\n      if (activeAt(st, x, y) && tileSolidType(t) && !tileSolidTopType(t)) return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** PumpkinSeeds/Boulders 共用收尾（:1077-1096 / :946-965） */\nfunction pumpkinOrBoulderScan(\n  env: SmartCursorEnv, reach: Region,\n  set: (x: number, y: number) => void,\n  pred: (st: TileStore, i: number, j: number) => boolean,\n): void {\n  const st = env.st;\n  const targets: Pt[] = [];\n  for (let i = reach.x0; i <= reach.x1; i++) {\n    for (let j = reach.y0; j <= reach.y1; j++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;\n      if (pred(st, i, j)) targets.push({ x: i, y: j });\n    }\n  }\n  const point = nearestOf(targets, env.mouse);\n  if (point && inTileBounds(point.x, point.y, reach.x0, reach.y0, reach.x1, reach.y1)) {\n    set(point.x, point.y);\n  }\n}\n"
}

4. stepHammers uses `pickNonAltar(st, targets, (t) => toWorld(t), p)` — p is actor which has cx/cy, not x/y. toWorld returns {x,y} and dist uses ref.x/ref.y. For the ray part vanilla uses providedInfo.Center (p.cx, p.cy) — so I need {x: p.cx, y: p.cy}. Fix.
5. `hitLineWall` returns Pt with -1 sentinel; ok.
6. In stepMineSolids: I shift endpoints while iterating pi over points — vanilla removes points[0] and endpoints[0] each loop, so both queues advance in lockstep. My version: `pi++` for points and `endpoints.shift()` — equivalent.
7. `st.inBounds(i, j, )` typo in stepAcorns — `st.inBounds(i, j, )` valid JS? `inBounds(x, y, )` with trailing comma in call is allowed in ES2017+. TS allows. But ugly — clean it.

  }

```
Note vanilla: color = Lighting.GetColor(x,y) then buffColor(newColor, 1, 0.9, 0.1, 1) * 0.6. buffColor multiplies newColor channels by r,g,b,a. So final = (R*1, G*0.9, B*0.1) * lightRGB * 0.6 alpha.

So fill: rgba(R*1*lr/255, G*0.9*lg/255, B*0.1*lb/255, 0.6) — vanilla alpha=1 then multiplied by num=0.6 → alpha 0.6.

Four quadrants: (0,0),(8,0),(0,8),(8,8) each 8×8 (scaled by zoom since drawn in screen space — use ts*z).

Border: color (1,0.95,0.3)*1 alpha 1: 
- left bar at (-2,0) size 2×16
- right at (16,0) 2×16
- top at (0,-2) 16×2
- bottom at (0,16) 16×2

Arrow: if lockedDirection and next tile in that direction is empty/replaceable: draw triangle at position = tileOrigin + dir*(16 or 20) + dir*(2-2*sin(phase)) px, phase = (time/0.9)%1. Draw in screen coords: convert world px to screen.

Rotation: frame index = SMART_CURSOR_DIRECTION_LOCKS.indexOf(dir); angle = index * 45° (clockwise from up). Since y axis in canvas points down and the locks array is (0,-1)=up first going clockwise (in screen coords where -y is up), angle in canvas radians = index * PI/4 works directly (canvas rotation positive = clockwise = matches).

Draw a triangle pointing along the direction: base triangle pointing up (0,-r)...(±w, +r), rotated by angle.

 "old_string": "  private toggle(label: string, get: () => boolean, set: (v: boolean) => void) {\n    const row = document.createElement('label');\n    row.className = 'sw-set-row';\n    const span = document.createElement('span');\n    span.textContent = label;\n    const input = document.createElement('input');\n    input.type = 'checkbox';\n    input.checked = get();\n    input.addEventListener('change', () => set(input.checked));\n    row.append(span, input);\n    return row;\n  }",
 "new_string": "  private toggle(label: string, get: () => boolean, set: (v: boolean) => void) {\n    const row = document.createElement('label');\n    row.className = 'sw-set-row';\n    const span = document.createElement('span');\n    span.textContent = label;\n    const input = document.createElement('input');\n    input.type = 'checkbox';\n    input.checked = get();\n    input.addEventListener('change', () => set(input.checked));\n    row.append(span, input);\n    return row;\n  }\n\n  /** 双态枚举行（点击在两态间切换；智能光标 Toggle/Hold 模式用） */\n  private modeRow(label: string, _get: () => string, value: () => string, cycle: () => void) {\n    const row = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = 'sw-set-row';\n    const span = document.createElement('span');\n    span.textContent = label;\n    const btn = document.createElement('button');\n    btn.className = 'sw-set-back';\n    btn.style.padding = '4px 14px';\n    btn.textContent = value();\n    btn.addEventListener('click', () => { cycle(); btn.textContent = value(); });\n    row.append(span, btn);\n    return row;\n  }"
}

 "command": "python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport json, collections\nfor culture, vals in {\n  'zh-Hans': {\n    'SmartCursorMode': '智能光标模式',\n    'SmartCursorToggle': '切换（按 Ctrl 开/关）',\n    'SmartCursorHold': '按住（按住 Ctrl 生效）',\n    'SmartAxeAfterPickaxe': '智能斧排在智能镐后',\n    'SmartBlocks': '智能方块放置',\n    'SmartHoldRelease': '按住模式：使用中松开即关',\n    'SmartCursorHint': '智能光标：按 Ctrl 自动选取挖掘/放置目标格（对齐原版 Ctrl 键）。',\n  },\n  'en-US': {\n    'SmartCursorMode': 'Smart Cursor Mode',\n    'SmartCursorToggle': 'Toggle (Ctrl to switch)',\n    'SmartCursorHold': 'Hold (hold Ctrl)',\n    'SmartAxeAfterPickaxe': 'Smart Axe After Smart Pickaxe',\n    'SmartBlocks': 'Smart Block Placement',\n    'SmartHoldRelease': 'Hold: release mid-use to cancel',\n    'SmartCursorHint': 'Smart Cursor: hold Ctrl to auto-target tiles for mining/placing (vanilla Ctrl key).',\n  },\n}.items():\n    p = f'~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/tools/l10n-custom/{culture}.json'\n    with open(p, encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        d = json.load(f, object_pairs_hook=collections.OrderedDict)\n    d['Mods']['SandboxWorld']['Settings'].update(vals)\n    with open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(d, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n        f.write('\\n')\n    print(culture, 'ok', list(d['Mods']['SandboxWorld']['Settings'])[-7:])\nEOF",
 "description": "写入 l10n-custom 双语键"

}));\n    expect(sc.lockedDirection).toBeNull();\n    expect(sc.lockedContinuity).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('八方向量化（GetDesiredDirectionFrom :1430）：右/上/右上', () => {\n    const sc = new SmartCursor();\n
